A[gr*N + gk] : 0.0;\n    }\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < BK*BN; idx += THREADS_X*THREADS_Y) {\n      int kk = idx / BN;\n      int c = idx % BN;\n      int gk = k0 + kk;\n      int gc = colStart + c;\n      Bs[kk][c] = (gk < N && gc < N) ? B[gk*N + gc] : 0.0;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    double afrag[TM], bfrag[TN];\n    #pragma unroll\n    for (int kk = 0; kk < BK; ++kk) {\n      #pragma unroll\n      for (int i=0;i<TM;i++) afrag[i] = As[kk][ty*TM+i];\n      #pragma unroll\n      for (int j=0;j<TN;j++) bfrag[j] = Bs[kk][tx*TN+j];\n      #pragma unroll\n      for (int i=0;i<TM;i++)\n        #pragma unroll\n        for (int j=0;j<TN;j++)\n          acc[i][j] += afrag[i]*bfrag[j];\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n  }\n\n  for i,j: gr=rowStart+ty*TM+i, gc=colStart+tx*TN+j; if in bounds C[gr*N+gc]=acc[i][j];\n}\n\nLoads from As use row r across kk - As[kk][r] contiguous in r for global; store pattern As[kk][r] with consecutive idx mapping: idx = r*BK+kk, so consecutive threads vary kk -> As[kk][r] strided by (BM+PAD) -> bank conflicts on store. j < N; j += blockDim.x) {\n        mat[static_cast<size_t>(i) * N + j] = static_cast<double>(v) / static_cast<double>(mod);\n        v += stride;\n        if (v >= mod) v -= mod;\n    }\n}\n\ntemplate <int BM, int BN, int BK, int TM, int TN>\n__global__ __launch_bounds__((BM / TM) * (BN / TN))\nvoid matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,\n                          const double* __restrict__ B,\n                          double* __restrict__ C,\n                          const int N) {\n    constexpr int TX = BN / TN;\n    constexpr int TY = BM / TM;\n    constexpr int NT = TX * TY;\n    constexpr int PAD = 1;\n\n    __shared__ double As[BK][BM + PAD];\n    __shared__ double Bs[BK][BN + PAD];\n\n    const int bx = blockIdx.x;\n    const int by = blockIdx.y;\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const int tid = ty * TX + tx;\n\n    const int rowStart = by * BM;\n    const int colStart = bx * BN;\n\n    double acc[TM][TN];\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int i = 0; i < TM; ++i)\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int j = 0; j < TN; ++j)\n            acc[i][j] = 0.0;\n\n    for (int k0 = 0; k0 < N; k0 += BK) {\n        for (int idx = tid; idx < BM * BK; idx += NT) {\n            const int r = idx / BK;\n            const int kk = idx - r * BK;\n            const int gr = rowStart + r;\n            const int gk = k0 + kk;\n            As[kk][r] = (gr < N && gk < N) ? ++i)\n#pragma unroll\n                for (int j = 0; j < TN; ++j)\n                    acc[i][j] += a[i] * b[j];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int i = 0; i < TM; ++i) {\n        const int gr = rowStart + ty * TM + i;\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int j = 0; j < TN; ++j) {\n            const int gc = colStart + tx * TN + j;\n            if (gr < N && gc < N)\n                C[static_cast<size_t>(gr) * N + gc] = acc[i][j];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\ntemplate <int BM, int BN, int BK, int TM, int TN>\nstatic void launchGemm(const double* dA, const double* dB, double* dC, const int N) {\n    dim3 block(BN / TN, BM / TM);\n    dim3 grid((N + BN - 1) / BN, (N + BM - 1) / BM);\n    matrixMultiplyKernel<BM, BN, BK, TM, TN><<<grid, block>>>(dA, dB, dC, N);\n}\n\nvoid matrixMultiply(const double* dA, const double* dB, double* dC, const size_t N) {\n    if (N == 0) return;\n    const int n = static_cast<int>(N);\n\n    if (n <= 1792) {\n        launchGemm<16, 16, 16, 2, 2>(dA, dB, dC, n);\n    } else {\n        launchGemm<32, 32, 32, 4, 4>(dA, dB, dC, n);\n    }\n}\n\n// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare\nbool false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n every element.\n__global__ void initMatrixKernel(double* __restrict__ mat, const int N) {\n    const unsigned long long mod = static_cast<unsigned long long>(N) * static_cast<unsigned long long>(N);\n     const double* __restrict__ B,\n                          double* __restrict__ C,\n                          const int N) {\n    constexpr int TX = BN / TN;\n    constexpr int TY = BM / TM;\n
